I’ll start trying to explain something simple, like what happened on an overnight shift, and then I’m suddenly talking about soccer, or paint colors, or patient charts. I can tell people are getting lost, and honestly I’m getting lost too.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually up or euphoric lately?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t felt unusually happy or energized like that. If anything, I’m just trying to get through what’s going on.\n\nDoctor: Have you been having beliefs that other people say aren’t true, but that feel definitely true to you?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I know people keep saying it’s just staffing shortages, but I don’t buy that. I’m sure my supervisors are changing my shifts on purpose, and they’re watching me through the unit cameras to see if I’m unsafe or going to mess up. It feels obvious to me, even when other people say that’s not what’s happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any seizures?\n\nPatient: No, not that I know of. I haven’t had anything like a seizure.\n\nDoctor: Have you been hearing voices when no one else is there?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t think I’ve heard voices like someone standing there talking to me when no one is there. At least, I haven’t noticed that.\n\nDoctor: Did these problems start after a head injury?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I can’t remember any head injury that started all this.\n\nDoctor: Did this start after you began a new medication or drug?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t remember it starting right after any new medication or drug.\n\nDoctor: Have you become much less motivated to do things than usual?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I used to actually do things—sketch murals, cook for my friends, play soccer on weekends. Now it just feels pointless, like I can’t make myself care. Most of the time I ignore messages and just stay in my apartment.\n\nDoctor: Have you been having episodes where you feel confused or not fully clearheaded?\n\nPatient: Yeah… kind of. I’ll read the same page of a patient note or even a bill three times and still not really know what it said. And little things, like picking what to buy at the store, can make me just freeze up. It’s like my head isn’t working right.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
